LOD streaming full-flow follow-up native audit

Executable: fnv_reverse/FalloutNV.exe, Fallout New Vegas 1.4.0.525
SHA-256: 42fee7d6cd74e801372aa89c8f71c974cebd3c20ec9ad43d1465b8fa9646b49c
Format: PE32 x86, image base 0x00400000
Research date: 2026-10-12
Interface: radare2 MCP, read-only disassembly, data, and xrefs

These are genuine tool-output excerpts from the supported executable.
Labels identify the inspected boundary; they are not runtime observations.
Automatic function prototypes, branch likelihoods, and data-xref access labels
are analysis annotations. ABI and ownership conclusions must follow the
instructions and verified native callers. Function xrefs are discovered direct
references, not proof of exhaustive indirect or mod-provided caller coverage.
No game execution or production edits were performed for this follow-up.
Interpretation and remaining unknowns belong to
  docs/lod_streaming_handoff_engine_fix_plan.md
